Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the spin structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 278. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 949 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by spin generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 278, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 949. QED.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the factorization result condition on spin structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 698. The convergence follows from the factorization result estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 986. QED.
